SELECT add_provenance('region');
SELECT set_prob(provenance(), prob) FROM region;

\.
SELECT add_provenance('partsupp');
SELECT set_prob(provenance(), prob) FROM partsupp;
CREATE VIEW european_nation AS SELECT DISTINCT t0.c1 AS c1 FROM nation t0, region t1 WHERE t1.c1 = t0.c3 AND t1.c2 = 'europe';
CREATE VIEW european_supplier AS SELECT DISTINCT t0.c1 AS c1 FROM supplier t0, european_nation t1 WHERE t1.c1 = t0.c2;
CREATE VIEW brass_part AS SELECT DISTINCT t0.c1 AS c1 FROM part t0 WHERE t0.c7 = 'brass' AND t0.c8 = 15;
CREATE VIEW q2_part AS SELECT DISTINCT t0.c1 AS c1 FROM brass_part t0, partsupp t1, european_supplier t2 WHERE t1.c1 = t0.c1 AND t2.c1 = t1.c2;
CREATE VIEW q2 AS SELECT DISTINCT 1 AS c1 FROM q2_part t0;
